    NOTES TO THE UNAUDITED INTERIM CONDENSED FINANCIAL STATEMENTS



    1.   一般資料                         1. GENERAL INFORMATION
    大新金融集團有限公司（ 「本公司」
                    ）與其附屬             Dah Sing Financial Holdings Limited (the “Company”) and its
    公司（統稱
        「本集團」）在香港、澳門及中國內地             subsidiaries (together the “Group”) provide banking, insurance,
    提供銀行、保險、金融及其他相關服務。                financial and other related services in Hong Kong, Macau, and the
                                      Chinese Mainland.

    2.   未經審核之財務報表及會計政策               2. U N A U D I T E D F I N A N C I A L S TAT E M E N T S A N D
                                         ACCOUNTING POLICIES
    載於本中期業績報告之資料不構成法定之綜               The information set out in this Interim Report does not constitute
    合財務報表。                            statutory consolidated financial statements.

    本中期業績報告之若干財務資料，乃摘錄自               Certain financial information in this Interim Report is extracted
    根據香港公司條例第622章第662(3)條及附表6         from the statutory consolidated financial statements for the
    第3部分已送呈公司註冊處及香港金融管理局              year ended 31 December 2025 (the “2025 consolidated financial

     11. 稅項                           11. TAXATION
     香港利得稅乃按照期內估計應課稅溢利以稅              Hong Kong profits tax has been provided at the rate of 16.5%
     率16.5%（ 2025年：16.5%）提撥準備。中國內地    (2025: 16.5%) on the estimated assessable profit for the period.
     及澳門稅款乃按期內估計應課稅溢利依本集              Taxation on profits in Chinese Mainland and Macau has been
     團經營業務地區之現行稅率計算。                  calculated on the estimated assessable profit for the period at the
                                      rates of taxation prevailing in these overseas markets in which the
                                      Group operates.

     遞延稅項是採用負債法就暫時差異，按預期              Deferred taxation is calculated in full on temporary differences
     該等稅項負債需清付時或資產可予扣減時所              under the liability method at the tax rates that are expected to
     適用之稅率作全數確認。                      apply in the period when the liability is settled or the asset is
                                      realised.

16. 虧損準備                                                          16. LOSS ALLOWANCE
下表提供按香港財務報告準則第9號本集團的                                              The tables below provide a reconciliation of the Groupʼs

28. 公平值體系                    28. FAIR VALUE HIERARCHY
本集團使用下列反映在釐定公平值中可觀察          The Group measures fair values using the following hierarchy that
及不可觀察參數重要性之體系計量公平值：          reflects the significance of the observable and unobservable inputs
                             used in the fair value measurement:

級別    內容                     Level         Descriptions

1     相同資產或負債於活躍市場中之         1             Quoted prices (unadjusted) in active markets for
      報價（未經調整）。本級別包括於                      identical assets or liabilities. This level includes
      交易所上市之權益性證券及衍生                       equity securities and derivatives that are listed on
      工具。                                  exchanges.

2     除第1級別所包括之報價外，其他        2             Inputs other than quoted prices included within
      資產或負債能直接（即價格）或間                      Level 1 that are observable for the asset or liability,
      接（即從價格導出）地可觀察之                       either directly (that is, as prices) or indirectly (that is,
      數據，該級別包括大多數場外交                       derived from prices). This level includes the majority
      易（
       「場外交易」）衍生工具合約。                      of the over-the-counter (“OTC”) derivative contracts.
      該級別之金融工具可通過具活躍                       These are financial instruments with quoted prices
      市場近似金融工具報價、非活躍                       for similar instruments in active markets or quoted
      市場的同等或類近的金融工具報                       prices for identical or similar instruments in inactive
      價，及通過具可觀察重要參數的                       markets and financial instruments valued using
      模型釐定金融產品的公平值。                        models where all significant inputs are observable.

3     資產或負債數據並非根據可觀察         3             Inputs for the asset or liability that are not based
      之市場數據（不可觀察之數據）。                      on observable market data (unobservable inputs).

30. 營業分項報告                    30. OPERATING SEGMENT REPORTING
本集團根據香港財務報告準則第8號《營運業          Segment reporting by the Group is prepared in accordance with
務分項》編製分項報告。向包括行政總裁及其          HKFRS 8 “Operating Segments”. Information reported to the chief
他執行委員會成員之主要營運決策人呈報而           operating decision maker, including the Chief Executive and other
作為資源分配及業績評估用途之資料，乃按           Executive Committee members, for the purposes of resource
銀行業務及保險業務之基礎來確定。本地銀           allocation and performance assessment, is determined on the
行業務之營運表現按業務活動分析，而中國           basis of banking business and insurance business. For banking
內地及澳門之銀行業務之營運表現按業務機           business, operating performances are analysed by business
構分析。對於保險業，資源配置和表現評價是          activities for local banking business, and on business entity
基於保險的企業實體的基礎。對於投資業務，          basis for banking businesses in Chinese Mainland and Macau.
營運表現是按業務機構進行分析的，這些機           For insurance business, resources allocation and performance
構被指定於控股公司層面投資和管理資金。           evaluation are based on insurance business entity basis. For
                              investment operations, operating performance is analysed on
                              business entity basis for those entities of the Group designated to
                              invest and manage funds at the holding companyʼs level.

經考慮到本地業務之客戶群、產品及服務，經          Considering the customer groups, products and services of local

下列呈報分項：                       splits the operating segments of the Group into the following
                              reportable segments:

    資服務。                          vehicle financing and credit card services, and the provision of
                                  insurance sales and investment services.

    資客戶主要是工商業及機構性客戶。              finance to commercial, industrial and institutional customers,

     就編製分項報告而言，對可直接認明為各個              For the purpose of segment reporting, revenue derived from
     別分項之源自客戶、產品及服務收入，將直接             customers, products and services directly identifiable with
     呈報於有關分項；而分項之間的資金運用及              individual segments are reported directly under respective
     資金資源所產生的收入和資金成本，按參照              segments, while revenue and funding cost arising from inter-
     市場利率之轉移價格機制分配至各分項。分              segment funding operation and funding resources are allocated
     項間之交易乃依據授予第三者或與第三者交              to segments by way of transfer pricing mechanism with reference
     易之同類條款定價。分項間之收入或支出於              to market interest rates. Transactions within segments are priced
     綜合賬內抵銷。為配合本集團主要營運決策              based on similar terms offered to or transacted with external
     人之業務分項績效審閱基準，利息收入已按              parties. Inter-segment income or expenses are eliminated on
     淨利息收入方式披露。                       consolidation. To align with the basis of reviewing business
                                      segment performance by the Groupʼs chief operating decision
                                      makers, interest revenue is disclosed in terms of net interest
                                      income.

     所有不同分項之直接開支將歸類於有關的分              All direct costs incurred by different segments are grouped under
     項分類。間接開支及支援部門開支乃依據開              respective segments. Indirect costs and support functionsʼ costs
     支性質，按耗用之時間及工作量和分項營運              are allocated to various segments and products based on effort
     收入，分配至不同的分項及產品。不能合理地             and time spent as well as segmentsʼ operating income depending
     分配至各分項、產品及支援部門之企業活動              on the nature of costs incurred. Costs related to corporate activities
     開支，則作企業開支呈列於
                「其他」項下。               that cannot be reasonably allocated to segments, products and
                                      support functions are grouped under Others as unallocated
                                      corporate expenses.

     國際債權資料是在考慮風險的轉移後，根據                             The information of international claims discloses exposures to
     交易對手的所在地而披露對外地交易對手最                             foreign counterparties on which the ultimate risk lies, and is
     終面對的風險。交易對手的所在地指其經常                             derived according to the location of the counterparties after taking
     居所地，通常為該交易對手經合法成立與登                             into account any transfer of risk. The location of counterparty is the
     記註冊之所在經濟體。一般而言，若交易對手                            residence of counterparty, which is normally the economy in which
     的債權是由在不同國家的另一方擔保，或履                             the counterparty is legally constituted and registered. In general,
     行債權是一間銀行的海外分行，而其總部是                             transfer of risk from one country to another is recognised if the
     處於不同的國家，才會確認風險由一國家轉                             claims against a counterparty are guaranteed by another party in
     移至另一國家。經計及任何認可之風險轉移                             a different country or if the claims are on an overseas branch of
     後，只有構成國際債權總額10%或以上之區域                           a bank whose head office is located in a different country. Only
     方作出披露。                                          regions constituting 10% or more of the aggregate international

     33. 風險管理                         33. RISK MANAGEMENT
     甲. 集團銀行系                         A.   Banking Group

     本集團明瞭各類風險會不停蛻變的特性並透              The Group recognises the changing nature of risk and manages it
     過完善的管理架構作有效管理。                   through a well-developed management structure.

     風險管理專注於信貸風險、市場風險、利率風             Risk management is focused on credit risk, market risk, interest
     險、流動資金風險、操作風險、聲譽風險及策             rate risk, liquidity risk, operational risk, reputation risk and
     略性風險。信貸風險之產生主要源於本集團              strategic risk. Credit risk occurs mainly in the Groupʼs credit
     之信貸組合，其中包括企業和零售借貸、汽車             portfolios comprising corporate and retail lending, vehicle
     貸款及財資和金融機構的批發借貸及財資投              financing, and treasury and financial institutions wholesale
     資。                               lending and treasury investment.

     大部份的市場風險乃源於財資及環球市場部              Market risk arises mainly from Treasury & Global Markets Division
     （「財資部」），主要與本集團資產負債表內             (“TRD”) and is associated principally with the Groupʼs on-and off-
     及資產負債表外之買賣交易及其證券投資有              balance sheet positions in the trading book and its investment
     關。                               securities.

     利率風險指因利率的不利變動而引致本集團              Interest rate risk means the risk to the Groupʼs financial condition
     的財政狀況面臨的風險。                      resulting from adverse movements in interest rates.

     流動資金風險乃指本集團未能在不衍生不可              Liquidity risk is the risk that the Group is unable to fund
     接受損失的情況下為新增的資產融資或就到              increases in assets or meet its payment obligations associated
     期之金融負債履行付款責任。                    with its financial liabilities when they fall due without incurring
                                      unacceptable losses.

     操作風險乃因內部程序、員工及系統之不足              Operational risk is the risk of loss (direct or indirect) resulting from
     與疏忽或外來的事件而產生之直接或間接虧              inadequate or failed internal processes, people and systems or
     損之風險。                            from external events.

     聲譽風險是指由於本集團之商業慣例、營運              Reputation risk is the risk arising from the potential that negative
     誤差或營運表現而可能帶來之負面宣傳風               publicity regarding the Groupʼs business practices, operational
     險。這些負面因素不論是否屬實，均可能令              errors or operating performance, whether true or not, could cause
     客戶產生憂慮或負面看法，削弱客戶基礎及              customer concerns or negative view, decline in the customer base
     市場佔有率或導致耗費龐大之訴訟或減少收              or market share, or lead to costly litigation or revenue reductions.
     入。

     策略性風險泛指由於差劣之策略性決定、不              Strategic risk generally refers to the corporate risk that may bring
     可接受之財務表現、策略施行上失當以及對              significant immediate or future negative impact on the financial
     於市場轉變欠缺有效應變而可能對集團財務              and market positions of the Group because of poor strategic
     及市場狀況有即時或日後重大負面影響之企              decisions, unacceptable financial performance, and inappropriate
     業風險。                             implementation of strategies and lack of effective response to the
                                      market changes.

  （甲）集團風險管理架構                       (a) The risk management structure of the Group

      績檢討（比對預測）、營運統計和政                   the Group Audit Committee (“AC”); review of key results

  •   比對已審閱的預算及分析主要非財               •    the review of financial performance by analysis against
      務指標的變化以檢討業績。                       approved budgets and analysis of variations in key non-
                                         financial measures.

  風險管理及合規委員會（「風險管理及合                The Risk Management and Compliance Committee (“RMCC”)
  規委員會」
      ）被委任監察及領導由集團風                 has been delegated the authority to oversee and guide the
  險部（
    「集團風險部」）和各功能委員會主                management of different risks which are more particularly

  時並保持足夠之流動資金以應付可能須                 longer periods at higher rates, while maintaining sufficient
  付之所有到期債務。                         liquidity to meet all claims that might fall due.

甲. 集團銀行系（續）                    A.   Banking Group (Continued)

  （己）市場風險                           (f) Market risk

  市場風險乃指由市場上利率及價格變化                 Market risk is the risk of losses in assets, liabilities and off-
  而引致對資產、負債及資產負債表外持                 balance sheet positions arising from movements in market
  倉之虧損風險。                           rates and prices.

  各類交易之市場風險均由董事會、風險                 Market risk exposure for different types of transactions is
  管理及合規委員會及財資及投資風險委                 managed within various risk limits and guidelines approved
  員會按董事會授予之權力所核准之各項                 by the Board, the RMCC and the TIRC under the authority
  風險限額及指引內處理。風險限額按組                 delegated from the Board. Risk limits are set at the portfolio
  合層面以及各產品及不同風險類別設                  level as well as by products and by different types of risks.
  定。該等限額綜合包含了名義金額、止蝕                The risk limits comprise a combination of notional, stop-
  限額、敏感性及運用市場風險數值（「市                loss, sensitivity and value-at-risk (“VaR”) controls. All trading
  場風險數值」）之監控。所有涉及市場風                positions are subject to daily mark-to-market valuation.
  險的交易持倉需要每日按市值入賬。集                 The Risk Management and Control Department (“RMCD”)
  團風險部之風險管理及監控部（「風險管                within the GRD, as an independent risk management and
  理及監控部」）乃一個獨立之風險管理                 control unit, identifies, measures, monitors and controls the
  及控制部門，負責比較風險和已審批限                 risk exposures against approved limits and initiates specific
  額，以識別、計量、監控及管理該等風險                actions to ensure positions are managed within an acceptable
  及提議具體行動去確保持倉被限制在可                 level. Any exceptions have to be reviewed and sanctioned by
  接受水平內。任何不符合限額情況均須                 the appropriate level of management of TIRC, RMCC or the
  依據有關政策及程序經合適管理層－財                 Board as stipulated in the relevant policies and procedures.
  資及投資風險委員會，風險管理及合規
  委員會或董事會審查及批准。

  大新銀行之附屬公司澳門商業銀行及大                 BCM and DSB China, which are subsidiaries of DSB, run their
  新銀行（中國）根據其一套自定限額和政                treasury functions locally under their own set of limits and
  策及在大新銀行設定之市場風險管理框                 policies and within the market risk management framework
  架內執行其本行之財資活動。大新銀行                 set by DSB. The RMCD of DSB oversees the market risk arising
  之風險管理及監控部監察及管理源自澳                 from the treasury operations of BCM and DSB China to ensure
  門商業銀行及大新銀行（中國）財資營運                that the risk controls put in place are commensurate with
  之市場風險，確保已實施的風險控制與                 their businesses and consistent with the Group standards.
  相關的業務同步並與集團的標準一致。

  本集團源自其交易賬及銀行賬之市場風                 The Group applies different risk management policies and
  險應用不同之風險管理政策及程序。                  procedures in respect of the market risk arising from its
                                    trading and banking books.

           本集團之交易賬內，在外匯、債務                     In the Groupʼs trading book, market risk is associated
           證券、權益性證券及衍生工具之交                     with trading positions in foreign exchange, debt
           易持倉中存在市場風險。                         securities, equity securities and derivatives.

                 (1) 市場風險計量方法                        (1) Market risk measurement technique

                 作為市場風險管理，本集團使                       In the management of market risk, the Group
                 用各種業界普遍採用之方法計                       measures market risks using various techniques
                 量市場風險及控制市場風險於                       commonly used by the industry and control market
                 設定之風險額度範圍內。主要                       risk exposures within established risk limits. The
                 用於計量及監控市場風險之計                       major measurement techniques used to measure
                 量方法概述如下。                            and control market risk are outlined below.

                   •   市場風險數值                            •    Value-at-risk

                   本集團應用市場風險數值                           The Group applies a VaR methodology, which
                   法，其乃一種以統計為基                           is a statistically based estimate, to measure
                   準就持作交易用途組合因                           the potential loss of its trading portfolio from
                   市場不利變化預計潛在損                           adverse market movements. It expresses as
                   失。其表明本集團於某個                           the maximum amount the Group might lose
                   置信水平可能損失之最大                           given a certain level of confidence, which
                   數額，就一日持倉期之基                           for the Group is 99% for a one day holding
                   準作推算，本集團之置信                           period. There is therefore a specified statistical
                   水平為99%。因此存在明                          probability that actual loss could be greater
                   確的統計概率，實際損失                           than the VaR estimate. Hence, the use of VaR
                   可能比市場風險數值之估                           does not prevent losses outside the VaR limits
                   計數為大。因此採納該方                           in the event of extreme market movements.
                   法並不能避免當市場狀況
                   發生重大變化時超逾此等
                   額度之損失。

                   市場風險數值模型假設某                           The VaR model assumes a certain “holding
                   個持倉期（就本集團而言                           period” (one day in the case of the Group)
                   為一天）
                      直至結束持倉。市                           until positions can be closed. It is calculated
                   場風險數值亦依據持倉之                           based on the current mark-to-market value
                   現時市值、市場風險因素                           of the positions, the historical correlation
                   過往在一個曆年週期之相                           and volatilities of the market risk factors over
                   互關係及波幅，使用一種                           an observation period of one calendar year
                   歷史模擬方法計算。                             using a method known as historical simulation
                                                         approach.

  （丑）金融資產及負債之公平值                    (l)   Fair values of financial assets and liabilities

  公平值受限於須由董事會負責確保本集                 Fair values are subject to a control framework that the
  團有適當的估值管治及控制程序之控制                 Board is held responsible for ensuring proper valuation
  框架。董事會授權財資及投資風險委員                 governance and control processes of the Group. It delegates
  會監管金融工具之估值程序。估值由風                 the responsibility for overseeing the valuation process for
  險管理及監控部和獨立專業合資格估值                 financial instruments to the TIRC. Valuation is performed
  師及精算師（如適用）獨立地進行，而估                independently by RMCD and where appropriate, by
  值結果乃定期驗證，確保公平值計量過                 independent and professionally qualified valuers and
  程之完整性。                            actuaries and the valuation results are periodically verified to
                                    ensure the integrity of the fair value measurement process.

  金融工具之公平值乃在目前市場情況下                 The fair value of financial instruments is the price that would
  市場參與者於計量日進行之有序交易中                 be received to sell an asset or paid to transfer a liability
  出售資產所收取或轉移負債所支付之價                 in an orderly transaction between market participants at
  格，不論該價格為直接可觀察或使用估                 the measurement date under current market conditions
  值方法估計。                            regardless of whether that price is directly observable or
                                    estimated using a valuation technique.

  凡金融工具之報價隨時且定期由交易                  A financial instrument is regarded as quoted in an active
  所、交易商、經紀人、行業組織、定價服                market if quoted prices are readily and regularly available
  務及監管機構發布，則被視作為活躍市                 from an exchange, dealer, broker, industry group, pricing
  場報價之金融工具。於活躍市場之報價                 service or regulatory agency. A quoted price in an active
  為公平值提供最可靠之證據，並須於可                 market provides the most reliable evidence of fair value and
  獲得時使用。倘金融資產或金融負債有                 shall be used whenever available. If a financial asset or a
  買入價及賣出價，本集團將採用買賣差                 financial liability has a bid price and an ask price, the price
  價中在該等情況下最能代表公平值之                  within the bid-ask spread that is most representative of fair
  價格。                               value in the circumstances is used by the Group.

  倘金融工具之可觀察市場報價未能直接                 Where observable market quotation of financial instruments
  獲得，本集團利用合適及獲廣泛認可之                 is not directly available, the Group estimates the fair value
  估值方法估計該等金融工具之公平值，                 of such financial instruments by using appropriate valuation
  包括現值方法及標準期權定價模型。於                 techniques that are widely recognised including present value
  應用該等金融工具之估值方法時，本集                 techniques and standard option pricing models. In applying
  團盡最大限度使用相關可觀察依據（例                 valuation techniques for these financial instruments, the
  如：利率、匯率、波動性、信貸息差） ，而              Group maximises the use of relevant observable inputs (for
  盡最少限度使用不可觀察依據。例如利                 examples, interest rates, foreign exchange rates, volatilities,
  率掉期合約之公平值按估計的未來現金                 credit spreads) and minimises the use of unobservable inputs.
  流之現值計算，遠期外匯合約之公平值                 For example, the fair value of interest-rate swaps is calculated
  一般根據現行遠期匯率計算，而期權合                 as the present value of the estimated future cash flows, the
  約之公平值則按合適之定價模型計算，                 fair value of foreign exchange forward contracts is generally
  如Black-Scholes模型。                 based on current forward rates and the fair value of option
                                    contracts is derived using appropriate pricing models, such as
                                    Black-Scholes model.

乙. 集團保險系                       B.   Insurance Group

本集團的保險業務涉及多種風險，包括保險            The Groupʼs insurance business is exposed to multiple risks,
風險、產品風險、投資風險及業務風險。本集           including insurance risk, product risk, investment risk and business
團相信有效的風險管理是控制及經營保險業            risks. We believe that effective risk management is an integral part
務的關鍵，有助維持本集團業務的盈利能力            of our insurance businessʼ control process and operations, and that
和穩健。                           effective control of risks assists to maintain the profitability and
                               stability of our business.

保險業務的主要風險及相關的控制程序              The key risks of our insurance business and related risk control
如下：                            process are as follows:

  （甲）保險風險                           (a) Insurance risk

  本集團的保險業務是承保有關保險的風                 The Groupʼs insurance operation is in the business of
  險，而所承保之各類別或事件的風險，視                underwriting insurance risk and retains various maximum
  乎風險的種類均設有最高保額，超額的                 amounts per risk or event dependent on the type of risk with
  風險將按不時檢討之各種轉保及相關協                 the excess being reinsured through various reinsurance and
  議分保。另外，保險集團亦採取分保作災                related agreements which are regularly reviewed. Catastrophe
  難補償安排以減低因特定事件索償（可                 cover arrangements are also in place whereby a number
  能涉及多項索償）對本集團的風險。                  of claims relating to a specific incident in aggregate would
                                    represent a material risk to the Group are reinsured.

  承保及索償方法及程序均需記錄及                   Underwriting and claims practices and procedures are
  檢討。                               documented and reviewed.

  （乙）產品風險                           (b) Product risk

  新產品及現有產品的重大修改須通過產                 New products and major revisions to existing products
  品認可程序，包括檢討產品的盈利能力                 undergo a product approval process with the profitability
  及如有需要交由內部及外部的獨立精算                 being reviewed and where appropriate assessed by internal
  師評核。                              and external independent actuaries.

  （丙）投資風險                           (c) Investment risk

  保險集團的投資方式是維持盡量平衡保                 Our investment practice is to maintain a conservatively
  險業務資產與對保單持有人的負債之回                 invested portfolio which attempts to maintain value whilst
  報、年期及貨幣的配合，並以保守投資組                matching assets and policyholder liabilities as appropriate, by
  合盡力保持投資價值，當中考慮的因素                 yield, duration and currency taking account of the associated
  包括相關的風險、稅務及監管規定。                  risks, taxation and regulatory requirements.
